It started after they moved us onto those rotating evening and overnight shifts at the bigger properties.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself getting easily distracted, fidgety, or having trouble staying focused on tasks, even outside of work or studying?\n\nPatient: Yeah… mostly the staying focused part. I’ll be in the middle of inventory and lose track, or go to the supply closet and forget what I went for. Same with studying—I'll keep reading the same line and it just doesn’t stick.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself struggling with staying organized, keeping track of tasks, or putting things off until the last minute?\n\nPatient: Yeah… keeping track of things is hard. I’ll be doing inventory and lose my place, or go to the supply closet and just… forget what I went in there for. Studying’s been the same. I read the same line over and over and it doesn’t really stick.\n\nDoctor: Do you have similar problems with concentration and forgetfulness in other places, like at home or when you’re out running errands, or is it mostly at work?\n\nPatient: It’s not just work. At home, if I’m trying to study for that supervisor certification, I’ll read the same line over and over and it just doesn’t stay. Errands… sometimes I’ll forget what I was going to grab. But I notice it most with inventory and the supply closet stuff.\n\nDoctor: Do you also have trouble concentrating or remembering things during social activities, conversations, or other situations outside of work and studying?\n\nPatient: A little, yeah. In conversations I can drift off and miss pieces, but… it’s more noticeable when I’m trying to do inventory or study. My head just doesn’t stay on task like it used to.\n\nDoctor: Looking back, did you have trouble focusing or staying on task when you were a child, like in school or during activities, or is this something that mostly started for you as an adult?\n\nPatient: I don’t really remember it being like this as a kid. I mean, maybe normal stuff, but… this feels more like now. I came in because my head just doesn’t stay on task anymore—inventory, studying for the supervisor certification, forgetting what I went to the supply closet for. That kind of thing.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I read the same line over and over and it doesn’t really stick.\n\nDoctor: Do you have similar problems with concentration and forgetfulness in other places, like at home or when you’re out running errands, or is it mostly at work?\n\nPatient: It’s not just work. At home, if I’m trying to study for that supervisor certification, I’ll read the same line over and over and it just doesn’t stay. Errands… sometimes I’ll forget what I was going to grab. But I notice it most with inventory and the supply closet stuff.\n\nDoctor: Do you also have trouble concentrating or remembering things during social activities, conversations, or other situations outside of work and studying?\n\nPatient: A little, yeah. In conversations I can drift off and miss pieces, but… it’s more noticeable when I’m trying to do inventory or study. My head just doesn’t stay on task like it used to.\n\nDoctor: Looking back, did you have trouble focusing or staying on task when you were a child, like in school or during activities, or is this something that mostly started for you as an adult?\n\nPatient: I don’t really remember it being like this as a kid.
